MrVI.  MrVI was trained on the  4,000 -HVG  raw  UMI count matrix with biological replicate 1463 

(batch_key = "replicate") registered as the technical nuisance batch covariate and compound 1464 

identity (sample_key = "compound_name") registered as the target sample covariate ( Ex-1465 

tended Data Fig. 3b, c ). Following training under  learning rate at 1e -4, minibatch size 512 ,1466 

and 2000 max epochs (early -stop enabled),  the sample -unaware basal latent representation  𝑢 1467 

and the sample -aware perturbation latent representation  𝑧  were obtained across all  62 ,207 1468 

cells and used to construct 15 -nearest -neighbor graphs and 2D UMAP embeddings. 1469 

MoCAVI.  MoCAVI was trained on the 4,000 -HVG raw UMI count matrix by partitioning the 1470 

dataset into an unperturbed background reference cohort ( 6,472  DMSO control cells) and a 1471 

perturbed target cohort ( 55 ,735  compound -treated cell s), with biological replicate supplied as 1472 

the categorical batch covariate ( Extended Data Fig. 3b, c ). Both the shared background en-1473 

coder (base) and the perturbation -specific salient encoder were parameterized as two -layer 128 -1474 

unit multi -layer perceptrons mapping onto 30 -dimensional latent spaces  under Wasserstein in-1475 

dependence regularization ( 𝜆 wasserstein  = 0.5). From the trained model, 30 -dimensional back-1476 

ground and salient embeddings were extracted to compute 15 -nearest -neighbor graphs and 2D 1477 

UMAP projections. Compound -level MoCAVI salient displacement magnitudes ( Fig. 3k )1478 

were quantified as the Euclidean ( 𝐿 2) distance between each compound's mean salient latent 1479 

centroid and the DMSO control salient centroid in  ℝ30 .1480 

PertVI.  PertVI was  trained on the  4,000 -HVG raw UMI count  matrix with  biological replicate 1481 

(batch_key = "replicate"), chemical perturbation identity (sample_key = "compound_name"), 1482 

and the binary treatment indicator (treatment_mask_key = "is_treated", set to  0 for the  6,472 1483 

DMSO control cells and  1 for the  55 ,735  compound -treated cells). The model was instantiated 1484 

with a 3 -dimensional basal and perturbation -modulated cell -cycle subspace ( 𝑢 cycle , 𝑧 cycle  ∈ ℝ3), 1485 

a 30 -dimensional basal and perturbation -modulated developmental state subspace 1486 

(𝑢 state , 𝑧 state  ∈ ℝ30 ), and a 5 -dimensional out -of -distribution anomaly subspace ( 𝑣 ood  ∈ ℝ5, an-1487 

chored at the origin in DMSO control cells via  binary treatment indicator ).  Subspace disentan-1488 

glement  was achieved through  regularization weights  at  𝜆 adv,state  = 100 .0 , 𝜆 adv,ood  = 50 .0 ,1489 

𝜆 ood  = 50 .0 (with a minimum floor of  20 .0), and soft cell -cycle gradient -starvation penalty 1490

𝜆 soft  = 50 .0. Using an 80% training ( 49 ,765  cells) and 20% validation ( 12 ,442  cells) split 1491

Latent space integration and disentanglement benchmarking.  We quantitatively benchmarked 1501 

12 latent embeddings (uncorrected Orig., ComBat, LDA, MrVI  𝑢 , MrVI  𝑧 , MoCAVI base, 1502

cycle phase (CC Phase, 3 classes), CD8+ T -cell developmental state (Cell State, 5 classes), and 1505 

chemical perturbation identity (Perturbation, 63 classes;  Extended Data Fig. 3b, c ). Clustering 1506 

separation and label conservation were evaluated via the adjusted F1 score ( aF1 ) using 5 -fold 1507 

stratified cross -validation with a 5 -nearest -neighbor classifier. Within each fold, the observed 1508 

macro -averaged F1 score ( F1 obs ) was calibrated against an empirical label -permutation null 1509 

baseline ( F1 null ) via  aF1  = max (0, (F1 obs  − F1 null )/(1 − F1 null )) and averaged across the five 1510 

folds. Local neighborhood mixing was quantified across all  62 ,207  cells using the Local In-1511 

verse Simpson ’s Index (harmonypy.compute_lisi, perplexity = 30) and normalized on a loga-1512 

rithmic scale. 1513

thapsigargin  0.670 , and sapanisertib  0.662 ). Enrichment of off -manifold displacement across 1648 

the four differentiation archetypes and across MoA classes was evaluated using two -sided 1649 

Fisher ’s exact tests with Benjamini -Hochberg FDR correction ( Fig. 3n and Extended Data 1650 

Fig. 7b ). To extract pure off -manifold gene signatures free of developmental state confounding, 1651 

we  used PertVI ’s Marginalized Counterfactual Operator (MCO), which integrates the  𝑣 ood 1652 

counterfactual intervention exclusively across the fixed, unperturbed DMSO vehicle control 1653 

cell distribution. Gene -level MCO log -fold -change signatures were  then  profiled across 18 ca-1654 

nonical oxidative, xenobiotic, endoplasmic reticulum stress, and interferon -stimulated cyto-1655 

lytic marker genes ( Extended Data Fig. 7c ). Pre -ranked Gene Set Enrichment Analysis 1656 

(gseapy.prerank, 1,000 permutations) was executed on the 4,000 -HVG MCO vector of each 1657 

compound against all 50 MSigDB Hallmark gene sets. Two -sided  Mann -Whitney  𝑈 tests with 1658 

Benjamini -Hochberg FDR control ( FDR  < 0.05 ) comparing Hallmark NES distributions be-1659 

tween the  𝑁  = 14  off -manifold compounds and  𝑁  = 48  on -manifold compounds identified 1660 

eight core differentially regulated stress and cell -cycle pathways ( Fig. 3o ), and unsupervised 1661 

Ward hierarchical clustering across the 48 Hallmark pathways stratified the 14 off -manifold 1662 

compounds into six mechanism -specific toxicity classes (Classes I –VI) across five functional 1663 

pathway programs ( Extended Data Fig. 7e, f ). 1664 

6.  Modeling on CD4+ T Cell Data 1665 

6.1  Data collection, subset, and preprocess 1666 

Pilot dataset creation.  Single -cell CRISPRi Perturb -seq data and accompanying arrayed CRIS-1667 

PRi bulk RNA -seq data from primary human CD4+ T cells were obtained from a published 1668 

genome -scale perturbation atlas 15  (GSE314342). The parent Perturb -seq atlas profiles 21.99 1669 

million single -guide primary human CD4+ T cells targeting 12,779 expressed genes (26,504 1670 

sgRNAs including 5% non -targeting controls) across four healthy blood donors (D1 –D4) and 1671 

three T -cell receptor (TCR; anti -CD3/CD28/CD2) stimulation conditions: unstimulated resting 1672 

baseline (Rest), acute activation at 8h post -stimulation (Stim 8hr), and late blastogenic effector 1673 

maturation at 48h post -stimulation (Stim 48hr), defining 111 co -functional perturbation clus-1674 

ters across conditions. To construct a balanced multi -condition benchmark dataset, we first 1675 

filtered out low -quality droplets and multi -guide transductions (~ low_quality and guide_id != 1676 

'multi -guide') and applied a water -filling stratified sampling algorithm across donors and 1677 

> preprint (which was not certified by peer review) is the author/funder. All rights reserved. No reuse allowed without permission. The copyright holder for this this version posted November 10, 2026. ;https://doi.org/10.64898/2026.11.07.765083 doi: bioRxiv preprint 38

conditions to assemble 120,545 high -quality single -guide cells spanning Rest (42,344 cells), 1678 

Stim 8hr (40,272 cells), and Stim 48hr (37,929 cells) across donors D1 (25,751 cells), D2 1679 

(41,133 cells), D3 (26,491 cells), and D4 (27,170 cells).  Overall, t he pilot dataset comprises 82 1680 

perturbation labels (81 gene -targeting knockdowns across 114,521 cells plus non -targeting 1681 

controls across 6,024 cells). Specifically, 61 curated T -cell activation regulators (77,003 cells) 1682 

were selected from the original study ’s Figure 3  HDBSCAN T -cell activation clusters (Cluster 1683 

21 at 8h, Cluster 22 at 8h and 48h, and Cluster 99 at 48h) . Based on their annotated  active 1684 

timepoints , these regulators  were stratified into  early - (active at 8h in Cluster 21,  𝑛  = 12  ;1685 

19,248 cells), general - (shared across 8h in Clusters 21 and 22 and 48h in Cluster 99,  𝑛  = 29 ;1686 

34,081 cells), and late - (active at 48h in Cluster 99,  𝑛  = 20 ; 23,674 cells) stage  regulators 1687 

spanning seven functional categories: T -cell activation, vesicle trafficking and cytoskeleton 1688 

dynamics, transcriptional regulators, cell cycle and genome integrity, metabolism, calcium ho-1689 

meostasis, and signaling. To establish an empirical null baseline alongside the curated regula-1690 

tors, 20 non -essential negative control genes (NCGs; 37,518 cells) were randomly sampled 1691 

from perturbed genes falling strictly outside all co -functional HDBSCAN clusters in the parent 1692 

atlas, and 6,024 non -targeting control (NTC) cells were uniformly sampled across the 12  do-1693 

nor -condition  pools (502 cells per pool, 2,008 cells per condition, and 1,506 cells per donor) to 1694 

match the 5.00% NTC proportion of the parent library. 1695 

Preprocess . We identified the top 10,000 donor -aware highly variable genes (HVGs) directly 1696 

from raw UMI counts using the Seurat v3 variance -stabilizing transformation (flavor="seu-1697 

rat_v3", n_top_genes =10000, batch_key="donor") implemented in Scanpy and formed their 1698 

union with 94 expressed canonical cell -cycle marker genes (42 S -phase and 52 G2/M -phase 1699 

markers). This union yielded 10,004 expressed genes comprising 9,910 non -cycle HVGs and 1700 

94 canonical cell -cycle markers. Raw UMI counts were preserved for  modeling . To  benchmark 1701 

canonical cell -cycle phase calling , phase  scoring (scanpy.tl.score_genes_cell_cycle) was exe-1702 

cuted independently within each stimulation condition on  library size -normalized and log -1703 

transformed expression (X_log1p)  to assign per -cell S  score, G2M  score, and discrete phase 1704 

labels (G1, S, and G2M). 1705 

6.2  Model training 1706 

PertVI.  PertVI was configured on the CD4+ T -cell dataset (120,545 cells  ×10,004 genes) with 1707 

a 3 -dimensional basal cell -cycle subspace ( 𝑢 cycle , 𝑧 cycle  ∈ ℝ3), a 30 -dimensional basal cell -state 1708 

subspace ( 𝑢 state , 𝑧 state  ∈ ℝ30  ), and a 5 -dimensional out -of -distribution subspace ( 𝑣 ood  ∈ ℝ5 ,1709 

configured with encoder dropout rate  0.3 and a baseline treatment mask set to  0.0 for resting 1710 

NTC cells and  1.0 otherwise). Subspace independence was enforced during training using 1711 

cross -subspace orthogonality regularization between  𝑢 cycle  and  𝑢 state  ( 𝜆 adv,state  = 100 .0 ,1712 

𝜆 adv,ood  = 20 .0) and an  𝐿 1 origin -anchoring penalty on  𝑣 ood (𝜆 ood  = 20 .0, floor  5.0). Using an 1713 

80% training (96,436 cells) and 20% validation (24,109 cells) split stratified jointly by pertur-1714 

bation label and donor (random seed 42), the model was trained  on raw UMI counts  for  20 00 1715 

epochs  (early -stop enabled)  using the Adam optimizer (learning rate  10 −4, minibatch size 512, 1716 

40 -epoch KL warmup, and 50 -epoch adversarial warmup followed by 100 -epoch annealing) 1717 

under a Negative Binomial count likelihood, with convergence monitored across total evidence 1718 

lower bound (ELBO), Negative Binomial reconstruction error, and Kullback -Leibler (KL) di-1719 

vergence ( Extended Data Fig. 8b ). Batch -corrected denoised expression (X_pertvi_log1p) 1720 

was computed by decoding normalized expression standardized to reference donor D2 (trans-1721 

form_batch="D2", scaled to  total sum at  10 4 ) followed by  log (1 + 𝑥 ) transformation. Uni-1722 

form Manifold Approximation and Projection (UMAP) embeddings were computed in Scanpy 1723 

(n_neighbors=15) across uncorrected PCA coordinates and each of PertVI's six latent repre-1724 

sentations ( 𝑢 cycle , 𝑢 state , 𝑧 cycle , 𝑧 state , 𝑣 ood , 𝑧 final ; Fig. 4b and Extended Data Fig. 8c ). 1725 

> preprint (which was not certified by peer review) is the author/funder. All rights reserved. No reuse allowed without permission. The copyright holder for this this version posted November 10, 2026. ;https://doi.org/10.64898/2026.11.07.765083 doi: bioRxiv preprint 39

scVI.  To benchmark PertVI against the single -cell generative embedding architecture used in 1726 

the original study 15 , we trained a dimensionality -matched, unconstrained 30 -dimensional scVI 1727 

model 30  (30D scVI) using scvi -tools 62  on the identical 120,545 cells and 10,004 genes ( Ex-1728 

tended Data Fig. 11a ). Matching the latent embedding protocol of the original study, the scVI 1729 

model was configured on raw UMI counts with donor batch conditioning (batch_key="donor", 1730 

encode_covariates=True) and was trained for  2000  epochs  (early -stop enabled)  with a mini-1731 

batch size of 2,048  and  learning rate  at  10 −4. All downstream visualization followed the same 1732 

settings used for PertVI. 1733 

6.3  Downstream analyses 1734 

Cell -cycle ring reconstruction and phase -zone differential abundance.  Within the 3 -dimen-1735 

sional basal cell -cycle subspace ( 𝑢 cycle ), where the first two principal components captured 1736 

99.94% of total variance as a planar ring ( Extended Data Fig. 8d ), continuous mitotic pro-1737 

gression was parameterized along a 6 -waypoint polar geodesic pseudotime ( 𝜏 cycle  ∈ [0, 1]; Fig. 1738 

4c, d and Extended Data Fig. 8e ). Six ordered biological waypoints ( 𝑊 0 to  𝑊 5) were defined 1739 

in 3D  𝑢 cycle  space from the quiescent Rest G1 centroid ( 𝑊 0), the activated Stim 8hr G1 centroid 1740 

(𝑊 1), early and late S -phase centroids ( 𝑊 2, 𝑊 3), and early and mitotic -peak G2/M centroids 1741 

(𝑊 4, 𝑊 5); cells were orthogonally projected onto the piecewise -linear polygonal curve  𝑊 0 →1742 

𝑊 1 → 𝑊 2 → 𝑊 3 → 𝑊 4 → 𝑊 5 with cumulative arc length quantile -scaled to  [0, 1]. Based on 1743 

inflection boundaries of canonical phase -marker expression,  𝜏 cycle  was partitioned into five se-1744 

quential mitotic phase zones: basal quiescence (C1: G0/G1,  [0.00 , 0.22 )), restriction -point li-1745 

censing (C2: Activated G1,  [0.22 , 0.40 )), early DNA synthesis (C3: Early S,  [0.40 , 0.58 )), late 1746 

DNA replication (C4: Late S,  [0.58 , 0.76 )), and mitotic division (C5: G2/M,  [0.76 , 1.00 ]). De-1747 

noised expression profiles (X_pertvi_log1p) of canonical cell -cycle markers were smoothed 1748 

across  𝜏 cycle  bins and grouped into five sequential transcriptional waves spanning G0/early -G1 1749 

quiescence (W1), G1/S restriction -point licensing (W2), S -phase DNA replication (W3), G2/M 1750 

checkpoint control (W4), and cytokinesis exit (W5). To verify that  𝑢 cycle  provides a perturba-1751 

tion -agnostic mitotic coordinate, trajectory conservation was quantified via Pearson correlation 1752 

(𝑟 ) between unperturbed NTC controls ( 𝑛  = 6,024 ) and all 120,545 cells at both the 5 -wave 1753 

composite level and the individual marker level ( Extended Data Fig. 8f, g ). For each pertur-1754 

bation and stimulation condition, within -condition phase -zone differential abundance ( log 2DA )1755 

across zones C1 –C5 was computed as the  log 2fold ratio of perturbed cell proportions to con-1756 

dition -matched NTC cell proportions within each zone (with pseudocount  𝜀  = 0.005 ), with 1757 

statistical significance assessed via two -sided Fisher ’s exact tests and corrected for multiple 1758 

hypothesis testing using the Benjamini -Hochberg false discovery rate ( FDR  < 0.05 ; Extended 1759 

Data Fig. 8h ). 1760 

Chronological Activation Pseudotime and state -zone differential abundance.  To quantify con-1761 

tinuous T -cell activation progression along the 30 -dimensional basal state manifold ( 𝑢 state ), we 1762 

constructed Chronological Activation Pseudotime (CPT,  𝜏 state  ∈ [0, 1] ; Fig. 4e, f and Ex-1763 

tended Data Fig. 9a, b ). Seven sequential biological reference landmarks ( 𝐿 0 to  𝐿 6) were de-1764 

fined in 30D  𝑢 state  space spanning the deep quiescent Rest NTC apex ( 𝐿 0), bulk Rest NTC 1765 

centroid ( 𝐿 1), Stim 8hr early -arrest and intermediate bridge centroids ( 𝐿 2, 𝐿 3), Stim 8hr acute 1766 

activation peak NTC centroid ( 𝐿 4), early -maturing Stim 48hr NTC centroid ( 𝐿 5), and mature 1767 

effector apex Stim 48hr NTC centroid ( 𝐿 6). Each cell was projected onto the piecewise -linear 1768 

geodesic  𝐿 0 → 𝐿 1 → ⋯ → 𝐿 6 via Gaussian distance -weighted softmin interpolation ( 𝜎 soft  =1769 

0.75 ) and quantile -normalized to  [0, 1], with monotonic alignment across Rest, 8hr, and 48hr 1770 

evaluated via Spearman rank correlation ( 𝜌  ) and three -condition Kruskal -Wallis  𝐻  -tests in 1771 

both NTC cells and all 120,545 cells. To map transcriptional dynamics along  𝜏 state , denoised 1772

CODE AVAILABILITY 1896 

The open -source PertVI Python package ( PertVI , v 3.1.0), built on PyTorch  and  scvi -tools 62 , is 1897 

publicly available on GitHub at  https://github.com/lenore -yue /PertVI . The repository provides 1898 

all custom Python scripts and Jupyter notebooks for synthetic benchmark generation, hyperpa-1899 

rameter sweeps, single -cell preprocessing, and baseline model comparisons (ComBat 64 , LDA, 1900 

scVI 30 , LinearSCVI 35 , MrVI 41 , ContrastiveVI 32 , DrVI 37 , and MoCAVI 42 . Scripts for down-1901 

stream trajectory, regulon, pathway, and differential expression analyses, and for reproducing 1902 

Figs. 1 –4 and  Extended Data Figs. 1 –11 , are included in the same repository. 1903

Fig . 1 | Architecture overview of PertVI. 2206 

a,  Conceptual motivation illustrating  th e variance entanglement problem and  on -/off -manifold  conflation  in 2207 

dynamic  cellular  systems . b,  Probabilistic graphical model of PertVI comparing variational inference 2208 

(dashed arrows) and the generative process (solid arrows). Latent variation is  partitioned  into on -manifold 2209 

perturbation -agnostic baseline anchors ( 𝑢 cycle  for cell cycle and  𝑢 state  for cell state) and an off -manifold per-2210 

turbation -blind anomaly  space  (𝑣 ood  for stress and toxicity). Observed perturbations  𝑝  modulate the baseline 2211 

anchors via a Split -FiLM transition module to yield the perturbation -aware cell representation  𝑧 , which com-2212 

bines with  𝑣 ood  and technical nuisance factors  𝑏  to generate  the  observed gene counts  𝑋 . c,  End -to -end neu-2213 

ral network architecture of PertVI. From the input raw count matrix  𝑋 , three parallel variational encoders 2214 

operating without access to perturbation or batch labels (Cycle Encoder, State Encoder, and Off -manifold 2215 

Encoder) infer the perturbation -agnostic baseline anchors  𝑢 : a circular cell -cycle ring  𝑢 cycle , a continuous 2216 

cellular state trajectory  𝑢 state , and an origin -anchored anomaly space  𝑣 ood  (where  control cells cluster at the 2217 

origin  (0, 0) and  perturbed cells disperse outward). A learned perturbation embedding  𝑒 𝑝  feeds into two par-2218 

allel Feature -wise Linear Modulation blocks (Cycle FiLM and State FiLM) that apply condition -specific 2219 

affine scaling and shifting (Control, Perturbation a, Perturbation b) to  𝑢 cycle  and  𝑢 state  to form the on -mani-2220 

fold perturbation representations  𝑧 cycle  and  𝑧 state  , whereas off -manifold perturbation effects  𝑣 ood  bypass 2221 

FiLM conditioning. In the bifurcated generative decoder,  𝑧 cycle  and the batch embedding  𝑒 𝑏  decode through 2222 

a masked linear Cycle Head restricted to canonical cell -cycle genes, while  𝑧 state , 𝑣 ood , and  𝑒 𝑏  decode through 2223 

a nonlinear Main Dense Head to reconstruct all trained transcriptomes.  d,  Downstream analysis in the per-2224 

turbation -agnostic anchor space  𝑢  across Control (grey), Perturbation a (red), and Perturbation b (blue) pop-2225 

ulations. Left (Cell Cycle,  𝑢 cycle  ): topology recovery along continuous circular pseudotime ( G1  → S →2226 

G2M ), where unrolled one -dimensional phase -density profiles resolve phase -specific occupancy shifts such 2227 

as Early G1 -arrest (Perturbation a) and Late M -arrest (Perturbation b). Right (Cell Differentiation,  𝑢 state ): 2228 

trajectory reconstruction along ordered developmental states ( State  0 → State  1 → State  2), resolving com-2229 

positional shifts such as State0 -arrest (Perturbation a) and State1 -promote (Perturbation b).  e,  Counterfactual 2230 

differential expression analysis in the on -manifold perturbation space  𝑧 . Left (Local Causal Score): at a fixed 2231 

local anchor on  𝑢 state , condition -specific FiLM transformations ( FiLM ctrl , FiLM pert  𝑎 , and  FiLM pert  𝑏 ) project 2232 

identical baseline cells onto  𝑧 state  to quantify the local counterfactual push  Δ(dashed arrows), which decodes 2233 

into a state -specific gene -level Causal Score vector. Right (Global Momentum Score): evaluating and inte-2234 

grating the counterfactual push  Δacross discretized unit steps along the  State  0 → State  1 trajectory on  𝑢 state 2235 

and decoding to gene space yields the trajectory -integrated gene -level Momentum Score vector for each 2236 

perturbation. 2237
